==================================================
FILE: export-project.ps1
==================================================


# ==========================================
# PROJECT EXPORTER
# Exports project files into one TXT file
# ==========================================

$ProjectPath = Get-Location
$OutputFile = Join-Path $ProjectPath "project-export.txt"

# Directories to ignore
$ExcludedDirectories = @(
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    ".vite",
    "coverage",
    ".next",
    ".idea",
    ".vscode"
)

# Files to ignore
$ExcludedFiles = @(
    "project-export.txt",
    "package-lock.json",
    "yarn.lock",
    "pnpm-lock.yaml",
    ".env",
    ".env.local"
)

# File extensions to include
$IncludedExtensions = @(
    ".html",
    ".css",
    ".scss",
    ".js",
    ".mjs",
    ".cjs",
    ".json",
    ".md",
    ".txt",
    ".xml",
    ".svg",
    ".yml",
    ".yaml",
    ".sql",
    ".ps1",
    ".ts",
    ".tsx",
    ".jsx",
    ".gitignore",
    ".config"
)

# Start export
"PROJECT EXPORT" | Out-File $OutputFile -Encoding utf8
"Project: $ProjectPath" | Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8
"Export date: $(Get-Date)" | Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

"`n========== PROJECT FILES ==========`n" |
    Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

# Get all files recursively
$Files = Get-ChildItem -Path $ProjectPath -Recurse -File |
    Where-Object {
        $RelativePath = $_.FullName.Substring($ProjectPath.Path.Length).TrimStart('\','/')

        $DirectoryExcluded = $false

        foreach ($Directory in $ExcludedDirectories) {
            if ($RelativePath -split '[/\\]' -contains $Directory) {
                $DirectoryExcluded = $true
                break
            }
        }

        $ExtensionIncluded = (
            $IncludedExtensions -contains $_.Extension -or
            $_.Name -in @(".gitignore", ".npmrc", ".prettierrc")
        )

        -not $DirectoryExcluded -and
        $_.Name -notin $ExcludedFiles -and
        $ExtensionIncluded
    } |
    Sort-Object FullName

# Export every file
foreach ($File in $Files) {

    $RelativePath = $File.FullName.Substring(
        $ProjectPath.Path.Length
    ).TrimStart('\','/')

    "`n`n==================================================" |
        Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

    "FILE: $RelativePath" |
        Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

    "==================================================`n" |
        Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

    Get-Content $File.FullName -Raw |
        Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8
}

"`n========== EXPORT COMPLETED ==========" |
    Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

"Total files exported: $($Files.Count)" |
    Out-File $OutputFile -Append -Encoding utf8

Write-Host " "
Write-Host "Export completed successfully!" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Files exported: $($Files.Count)"
Write-Host "Output: $OutputFile"

FILE: README.md

# BYU-Pathway Worldwide Online

## WDD 330 - Web Frontend Development II

### в›є SleepOutside Starter Code

- This repository is the start of the SleepOutside web application project for WDD 330. The repository contains branches which are checkpoints for the team and individual assignments throughout the course.

- <https://byui-cse.github.io/wdd330-ww-course/week01/team.html>

### Prerequisites

- You must have Node installed to run the following commands.
[WDD 330 Setup Environment](https://byui-cse.github.io/wdd330-ww-course/intro/)

### Common Workflow Commands

- `npm run lint` to run ESLint against your code to find errors.
- `npm run format` to run Prettier to automatically format your code.
- `npm run start` starts up a local server and updates on any JS or CSS/SCSS
- `npm run build` to build final files when you are ready to turn in.

---
_BYU-Pathway Worldwide improves lives through access to spiritually based, online affordable higher education. Its mission is to develop disciples of Jesus Christ who are leaders in their homes, the Church, and their communities._

  --small-font: 0.8em;
  --large-font: 1.2em;

FILE: src\js\Alert.js

export default class Alert {
  constructor(alertsUrl = "/json/alerts.json") {
    this.alertsUrl = alertsUrl;
  }

  async init() {
    const response = await fetch(this.alertsUrl);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unable to load alerts: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`,
      );
    }

    const alerts = await response.json();

    if (alerts.length > 0) {
      this.render(alerts);
    }
  }

  render(alerts) {
    const alertList = document.createElement("section");
    alertList.className = "alert-list";

    alerts.forEach(({ message, background, color }) => {
      const alertMessage = document.createElement("p");
      alertMessage.textContent = message;
      alertMessage.style.backgroundColor = background;
      alertMessage.style.color = color;
      alertList.append(alertMessage);
    });

    document.querySelector("main").prepend(alertList);
  }
}

FILE: src\js\CheckoutProcess.mjs
==================================================

import { getLocalStorage } from "./utils.mjs";
import ExternalServices from "./ExternalServices.mjs";

function packageItems(items) {
    return items.map((item) => ({
        id: item.Id,
        name: item.Name,
        price: item.FinalPrice,
        quantity: item.quantity
    }));
}

function formDataToJSON(formElement) {
    const formData = new FormData(formElement);
    const convertedJSON = {};
    formData.forEach((value, key) => {
        convertedJSON[key] = value;
    });
    return convertedJSON;
}

export default class CheckoutProcess {
    constructor(key, outputSelector) {
        this.key = key;
        this.outputSelector = outputSelector;
        this.list = [];
        this.itemTotal = 0;
        this.shipping = 0;
        this.tax = 0;
        this.orderTotal = 0;
    }

    init() {
        this.list = getLocalStorage(this.key);
        this.calculateItemSubTotal();
    }

    calculateItemSubTotal() {
        this.itemTotal = this.list.reduce(
            (sum, item) => sum + item.FinalPrice * item.quantity, 0);
        document.querySelector("#subtotal").textContent = this.itemTotal.toFixed(2);
    }

    calculateOrderTotal() {
        const count = this.list.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
        this.tax = (this.itemTotal * 0.06);
        this.shipping = 10 + (count - 1) * 2;
        this.orderTotal = this.itemTotal + this.tax + this.shipping;

        this.displayOrderTotals()
    }

    displayOrderTotals() {
        document.querySelector("#tax").textContent = this.tax.toFixed(2);
        document.querySelector("#shipping").textContent = this.shipping.toFixed(2);
        document.querySelector("#orderTotal").textContent = this.orderTotal.toFixed(2);
    }

    async checkout(form) {
        const order = formDataToJSON(form);
        order.orderDate = new Date().toISOString();
        order.orderTotal = this.orderTotal.toFixed(2);
        order.tax = this.tax.toFixed(2);
        order.shipping = this.shipping;
        order.items = packageItems(this.list);


        const response = await new ExternalServices().checkout(order);
        console.log(response);
    }

}



==================================================
FILE: src\js\ExternalServices.mjs
==================================================

const baseURL = import.meta.env.VITE_SERVER_URL;
const categories = ["tents", "backpacks", "sleeping-bags", "hammocks"];

function convertToJson(res) {
  if (res.ok) {
    return res.json();
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Request failed: ${res.status} ${res.statusText}`);
  }
}

export default class ExternalServices {
  async getData(query) {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${baseURL}products/search/${encodeURIComponent(query)}`,
    );
    const data = await convertToJson(response);
    return Array.isArray(data.Result) ? data.Result : [];
  }

  async searchProducts(query) {
    const productsByCategory = await Promise.all(
      categories.map((category) => this.getData(category)),
    );
    const normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLowerCase();

    return productsByCategory
      .flat()
      .filter((product) =>
        [
          product.Name,
          product.NameWithoutBrand,
          product.Brand?.Name,
        ].some((value) => value?.toLowerCase().includes(normalizedQuery)),
      );
  }

  async findProductById(id) {
    const response = await fetch(`${baseURL}product/${id}`);
    const data = await convertToJson(response);
    return data.Result;
  }

  async checkout(payload) {
    const options = {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    };

    return await fetch(`${baseURL}checkout`, options).then(convertToJson);
  }
}

import { getLocalStorage, setLocalStorage, updateCartCount } from "./utils.mjs";

export default class ProductDetails {

    constructor(productId, dataSource) {
        this.productId = productId;
        this.product = {};
        this.dataSource = dataSource;
        this.addButtonText = "";
        this.cartFeedbackTimeout = null;
    }

    async init() {
        // use the datasource to get the details for the current product. findProductById will return a promise! use await or .then() to process it
        this.product = await this.dataSource.findProductById(this.productId);
        // the product details are needed before rendering the HTML
        this.renderProductDetails();
        // once the HTML is rendered, add a listener to the Add to Cart button
        // Notice the .bind(this). This callback will not work if the bind(this) is missing. Review the readings from this week on 'this' to understand why.
        
        const breadcrumb =
            document.querySelector("#breadcrumb");

        if (breadcrumb) {
            breadcrumb.innerHTML =
                `<a href="/index.html">Home</a> в†’ ${this.product.NameWithoutBrand}`;
        }
        
        const addButton = document.getElementById("addToCart");

        this.addButtonText = addButton.textContent.trim();

        addButton.addEventListener(
            "click",
            this.addProductToCart.bind(this),
        );
    }

    addProductToCart() {
        const cartContents = getLocalStorage("so-cart") || [];
        const existingItem = cartContents.find(
            (item) => item.Id === this.product.Id,
        );

        if (existingItem) {
            existingItem.quantity = Number(existingItem.quantity) || 1;
            existingItem.quantity += 1;
        } else {
            cartContents.push({
                ...this.product,
                quantity: 1,
            });
        }

        setLocalStorage("so-cart", cartContents);
        updateCartCount();

        const button = document.getElementById("addToCart");
        const confirmation = document.querySelector("#cart-confirmation");

        clearTimeout(this.cartFeedbackTimeout);

        button.textContent = "In the Cart";
        confirmation.textContent =
            `${this.product.NameWithoutBrand} added to your cart!`;

        confirmation.classList.add("is-visible");

        this.cartFeedbackTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
            button.textContent = this.addButtonText;
            confirmation.classList.remove("is-visible");
        }, 3000);
    }

    renderProductDetails() {
        productDetailsTemplate(this.product);
    }
}

function productDetailsTemplate(product) {
   
    const isDiscounted = product.FinalPrice < product.SuggestedRetailPrice; 

    const discount = Math.round(
        ((product.SuggestedRetailPrice - product.FinalPrice) /
            product.SuggestedRetailPrice) *
        100
    );

    document.querySelector("h2").textContent = product.Brand.Name;
    document.querySelector("h3").textContent = product.NameWithoutBrand;

    const productImage = document.getElementById("productImage");
    productImage.alt = product.NameWithoutBrand;
    productImage.onload = () => {
        productImage.classList.remove("product-image--loading");
    };
    productImage.onerror = () => {
        productImage.classList.remove("product-image--loading");
        productImage.alt = `${product.NameWithoutBrand} image unavailable`;
    };
    if (product.Images) {
        productImage.srcset = `${product.Images.PrimaryMedium} 160w, ${product.Images.PrimaryLarge} 320w, ${product.Images.PrimaryExtraLarge} 600w`;
        productImage.sizes = "(max-width: 600px) 90vw, 600px";
        productImage.src = product.Images.PrimaryLarge;
    } else {
        productImage.src = product.Image;
    }

    document.getElementById("productPrice").innerHTML = `MSRP $${product.SuggestedRetailPrice.toFixed(2)}`;

    document.getElementById("productDiscount").innerHTML =  isDiscounted
        ? `<span class="discount-badge">${discount}% OFF</span>`
        : "";

    document.getElementById("productDiscountPrice").innerHTML = `Our Price $${product.FinalPrice.toFixed(2)}`;

    document.getElementById("productColor").textContent =
        product.Colors[0].ColorName;
    document.getElementById("productDesc").innerHTML =
        product.DescriptionHtmlSimple;
    document.getElementById("addToCart").dataset.id = product.Id;
}

import { renderListWithTemplate } from "./utils.mjs";

function productCardTemplate(product) {
  const isDiscounted = product.FinalPrice < product.SuggestedRetailPrice;
  const discountPercent = isDiscounted
    ? Math.round(
        ((product.SuggestedRetailPrice - product.FinalPrice) /
          product.SuggestedRetailPrice) *
          100
      )
    : 0;

  const images = product.Images || {};
  const imageSrc = images.PrimaryMedium || product.Image;
  const imageSrcset = images.PrimarySmall
    ? `srcset="${images.PrimarySmall} 80w, ${images.PrimaryMedium} 160w, ${images.PrimaryLarge} 320w" sizes="(max-width: 500px) 160px, 320px"`
    : "";

  return `
      <li class="product-card">
        <a href="../product_pages/index.html?product=${product.Id}">
          <img ${imageSrcset} src="${imageSrc}" alt="${product.NameWithoutBrand}">
          <h3 class="card__brand"> ${product.Brand.Name} </h3>
          <h2 class="card__name"> ${product.NameWithoutBrand} </h2>
          <p class="product-card__price">
            $${product.FinalPrice}
            ${
              isDiscounted
                ? `<span class="product-card__original-price">$${product.SuggestedRetailPrice.toFixed(2)}</span>`
                : ""
            }
          </p>
          ${
            isDiscounted
              ? `<span class="discount-badge">${discountPercent}% OFF</span>`
              : ""
          }
        </a>
      </li>
    `;
}

export default class ProductList {

  constructor(query, dataSource, listElement, isSearch = false) {
    this.query = query;
    this.dataSource = dataSource;
    this.listElement = listElement;
    this.products = [];
    this.isSearch = isSearch;
  }

  async init() {
    this.products = this.isSearch
      ? await this.dataSource.searchProducts(this.query)
      : await this.dataSource.getData(this.query);

    // Render the initial unsorted list
    this.renderList(this.products);
  }

  sortList(criterion) {
    let sortedList = [...this.products];

    if (criterion === "name") {
      sortedList.sort((a,b) => a.NameWithoutBrand.localeCompare(b.NameWithoutBrand));
    } else if (criterion === "price") {
      sortedList.sort((a,b) => a.FinalPrice - b.FinalPrice);
    }

    // Re-render the list container with the newly sorted items
    this.renderList(sortedList);
  }

  renderList(list) {
    // Clear out current contents to prevent items from duplicating or stacking
    this.listElement.innerHTML = "";

    if (list.length === 0) {
      this.listElement.innerHTML =
        "<li class=\"no-results\">No products found.</li>";
      return;
    }

    renderListWithTemplate(
      productCardTemplate,
      this.listElement,
      list
    );
  }
}

FILE: src\js\ShoppingCart.mjs

import {
  getLocalStorage,
  setLocalStorage,
  renderListWithTemplate,
  updateCartCount,
} from "./utils.mjs";


function getCartImageUrl(item) {
  return item.Images?.PrimaryMedium || item.Image;
}

function cartItemTemplate(item) {
  return `
    <li class="cart-card divider">

      <a href="#" class="cart-card__image">
        <img
          src="${getCartImageUrl(item)}"
          alt="${item.Name}"
        />
      </a>

      <div class="cart-card__details">
        <a href="#">
          <h2 class="card__name">${item.Name}</h2>
        </a>

        <p class="cart-card__color">
          ${item.Colors[0].ColorName}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="cart-card__quantity">
        <span>Qty: ${item.quantity}</span>
        <span class="cart-card__quantity-controls">
          <button
            type="button"
            class="cart-card_quantity-button"
            data-action="decrease"
            data-id="${item.Id}"
            aria-label="Decrease quantity of ${item.Name}"
            ${item.quantity === 1 ? "disabled" : ""}
          >
            в€’
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="cart-card_quantity-button"
            data-action="increase"
            data-id="${item.Id}"
            aria-label="Increase quantity of ${item.Name}"
          >
            +
          </button>
        </span>
      </div>

      <div class="cart-card__actions">
        <p class="cart-card__price">$${(
          Number(item.FinalPrice) * item.quantity
        ).toFixed(2)}</p>

        <button
          type="button"
          class="cart-card_remove"
          data-id="${item.Id}"
          aria-label="Remove ${item.Name} from cart"
        >
          Remove
        </button>
      </div>

    </li>
  `;
}

export default class ShoppingCart {
  constructor(listElement) {
    this.listElement = listElement;

    this.cartItems = (getLocalStorage("so-cart") || []).map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      quantity: Number(item.quantity) || 1,
    }));
    this.statusTimeout = null;
  }

  init() {
    this.renderCartContents();
  }

  renderCartContents() {
    renderListWithTemplate(
      cartItemTemplate,
      this.listElement,
      this.cartItems,
      "afterbegin",
      true
    );

    this.renderCartTotal();
    document.querySelector("#empty-cart-message").hidden =
      this.cartItems.length > 0;

    document
      .querySelectorAll(".cart-card_remove")
      .forEach((button) => {
        button.addEventListener(
          "click",
          (event) => {
            const id = event.target.dataset.id;

            this.removeItemFromCart(id);
          }
        );
      });

    document
      .querySelectorAll(".cart-card_quantity-button")
      .forEach((button) => {
        button.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
          const target = event.currentTarget;
          const id = target.dataset.id;
          const amount = target.dataset.action === "increase" ? 1 : -1;

          this.updateItemQuantity(id, amount);
        });
      });
  }

  renderCartTotal() {
    const footer =
      document.querySelector(".cart-footer");

    if (!footer) return;

    if (this.cartItems.length === 0) {
      footer.classList.add("hide");
      return;
    }

    const total = this.cartItems.reduce(
      (sum, item) =>
        sum + Number(item.FinalPrice) * item.quantity,
      0
    );

    document.querySelector(".cart-total").textContent =
      `Total: $${total.toFixed(2)}`;

    footer.classList.remove("hide");
  }

  removeItemFromCart(id) {
    const index =
      this.cartItems.findIndex(
        (item) => item.Id === id
      );

    if (index < 0) {
      return;
    }

    const removedItem = this.cartItems[index];
    this.cartItems.splice(index, 1);

    setLocalStorage("so-cart", this.cartItems);
    updateCartCount();
    this.showStatus(`${removedItem.Name} was removed from your cart.`);

    this.renderCartContents();
  }

  updateItemQuantity(id, amount) {
    const item = this.cartItems.find((cartItem) => cartItem.Id === id);

    if (!item) {
      return;
    }

    const nextQuantity = item.quantity + amount;

    if (nextQuantity < 1) {
      return;
    }

    item.quantity = nextQuantity;
    setLocalStorage("so-cart", this.cartItems);
    updateCartCount();
    this.renderCartContents();
  }

  showStatus(message) {
    const status = document.querySelector("#cart-status");

    if (!status) {
      return;
    }

    clearTimeout(this.statusTimeout);
    status.textContent = message;
    status.classList.add("is-visible");

    this.statusTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      status.textContent = "";
      status.classList.remove("is-visible");
    }, 3500);
  }
}


==================================================
FILE: src\js\theme.js
==================================================


const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = "sleepoutside-theme-mode";
const AUTO_MODE = "auto";
const DARK_MODE = "dark";
const LIGHT_MODE = "light";

function getAutomaticTheme() {
  const now = new Date();
  const currentMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();

  // Dark mode: 10 PM (22:00) - 6 AM (06:00)
  if (currentMinutes >= 22 * 60 || currentMinutes < 6 * 60) {
    return DARK_MODE;
  }

  return LIGHT_MODE;
}

function getSavedMode() {
  const savedMode = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);

  if ([AUTO_MODE, DARK_MODE, LIGHT_MODE].includes(savedMode)) {
    return savedMode;
  }

  return AUTO_MODE;
}

function applyTheme() {
  const mode = getSavedMode();

  const theme =
    mode === AUTO_MODE ? getAutomaticTheme() : mode;

  document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);

  const button = document.querySelector("#theme-toggle");

  if (button) {
    const labels = {
      auto: "Theme: Auto",
      dark: "Theme: Dark",
      light: "Theme: Light",
    };

    button.textContent = labels[mode];
    button.setAttribute("aria-label", `Color theme setting: ${mode}`);
    button.setAttribute("title", "Click to change color theme");
  }
}

function createThemeButton() {
  const header = document.querySelector("#main-header");

  if (!header || document.querySelector("#theme-toggle")) {
    return;
  }

  const button = document.createElement("button");

  button.id = "theme-toggle";
  button.className = "theme-toggle";
  button.type = "button";

  button.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const currentMode = getSavedMode();

    const nextMode = {
      auto: DARK_MODE,
      dark: LIGHT_MODE,
      light: AUTO_MODE,
    };

    localStorage.setItem(
      THEME_STORAGE_KEY,
      nextMode[currentMode],
    );

    applyTheme();
  });

  header.append(button);
}

export function initTheme() {
  // Apply the saved theme immediately
  applyTheme();

  // Create the button when the shared header is available
  createThemeButton();

  // Recheck automatic mode when the local time changes
  if (!window.themeTimer) {
    window.themeTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (getSavedMode() === AUTO_MODE) {
        applyTheme();
      }
    }, 60000);
  }

  // Recheck when returning to the browser tab
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
    if (!document.hidden && getSavedMode() === AUTO_MODE) {
      applyTheme();
    }
  });
}



==================================================
FILE: src\js\utils.mjs
==================================================

import { initTheme } from "./theme.js";

// wrapper for querySelector...returns matching element
export function qs(selector, parent = document) {
  return parent.querySelector(selector);
}

// retrieve data from localstorage
export function getLocalStorage(key) {
  return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));
}
// save data to local storage
export function setLocalStorage(key, data) {
  localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
}
// set a listener for both touchend and click
export function setClick(selector, callback) {
  qs(selector).addEventListener("touchend", (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    callback();
  });
  qs(selector).addEventListener("click", callback);
}

// NEW: get a parameter value from the URL query string
export function getParam(param) {
  const queryString = window.location.search;
  const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(queryString);
  return urlParams.get(param);
}

// render a list of items using a template function
export function renderListWithTemplate(
  templateFn,
  parentElement,
  list,
  position = "afterbegin",
  clear = false
) {
  if (clear) {
    parentElement.innerHTML = "";
  }
  const htmlStrings = list.map(templateFn);
  parentElement.insertAdjacentHTML(position, htmlStrings.join(""));
}

//sets the text to reflect the number of items in the cart(Ivan updated)
export function updateCartCount() {
  const cart = getLocalStorage("so-cart") || [];
  const countElement = document.querySelector(".cart-count");

  if (!countElement) return;

  const count = cart.reduce(
    (total, item) => total + (Number(item.quantity) || 1),
    0,
  );

  countElement.textContent = count > 0 ? count : "";
  countElement.hidden = count === 0;
}

export function renderWithTemplate(template, parentElement, data, callback,) {
  parentElement.innerHTML = template;

  if (callback) {
    callback(data);
  }
}

export async function loadTemplate(path) {
  const res = await fetch(path);
  const template = await res.text();
  return template;
}

// There may be a issue finding the partials folder . . . if that is the case, try ./partials (Ivan Updated)


export async function loadHeaderFooter() {
  // Apply the theme before loading shared components
  initTheme();

  const headerTemplate = await loadTemplate("/partials/header.html");
  const footerTemplate = await loadTemplate("/partials/footer.html");

  const headerElement = document.querySelector("#main-header");
  const footerElement = document.querySelector("#main-footer");

  if (headerElement) {
    renderWithTemplate(headerTemplate, headerElement);
    updateCartCount();

    // Add the theme button after loading the header
    initTheme();
  }

  if (footerElement) {
    renderWithTemplate(footerTemplate, footerElement);
  }
}
